[d101] SAY: ◈ THE AUDIT — the System reads your run back to you, and the galaxy listens. A lesser seat — global standing, waves 1–3 faced (12/7/3), 11 contests survived, 3 crises weathered. The table does not rise. It prices.
[d101] SAY: The table notes 1 ascension from one village — its terms improve accordingly. Nobody has ever seen that before.
[d101] SYS: EVERYONE. SIT. WATCH. The teeth and the weather can WAIT — nothing out there matters until this is done. ...Did I stutter? SIT.
[d101] SAY: ◈ ABILITY — Dreadfield: The room feels you before you speak. Alien-terror starts at 2.
[d101] SAY: ◈ ABILITY — War-table: You read the table's fear the way you read weather. The Nielsen flinches first — you saw it.
[d101] SAY: ◈ ABILITY — Oathbound: Your terror is pledged as a shield: your own people's horror grows half as fast.
[d101] SAY: ◈ ABILITY — Shieldbearer: Spend terror to calm horror — the shield, not the sword. (Offered in the final beat.)
[d101] SYS: 🔴 LIVE! the terror broadcast: WHAT EATING BUILT
[d101] SAY: ◈ THE FEARED — "Look what eating built." The footage plays unasked — the deer, the fire, the ones who came for you and didn't come back. Nobody at the table is comfortable. That is the point.
[d101] SAY: The table has read your ledger. The food truth was shared. The light road is open.
[d101] SAY: The deer. The cleanest kill. The table goes very still. Behind you, your own people go still too — a different stillness.
[d101] SAY: You let them see it. All of it. The table recalculates. The fire back home burns a little lower.
[d101] SAY: You spend it — the shield, not the sword. The small envoy stares. Your people stand a little straighter. Protective terror: the table writes it down and doesn't understand it, which is the point.
[d101] SAY: ◈ THE FEARED — the last choice is yours, and it's live.
[d101] SAY: ◈ THE FEARED — "Show mercy."
[d101] SAY: The most frightening thing you ever did. The table doesn't know what to do with mercy from a predator. Neither do you, quite. But the audience weeps, and the galaxy learns that the feared one chose gentleness — and that choice echoes longer than any threat. The terror stays, pledged now: a shield over every village, forever.
[d101] SAY: ◈ OUTSHONE — Victor Albrecht did what you would not: acted, boldly, while there was still time. The village thrives. The god is someone else. The record notes, kindly and accurately, that you watched. It stings exactly as much as it should.
[d101] SAY: The terms held — the table had priced the village, ascension and all, and the village paid in full.
[d101] SAY: ◈ THE OVERSIGHT —
[d101] SAY: You won. Say it plainly: you won. The village endures. The table signed. The galaxy applauds, and the applause is real, and you earned it.
[d101] SAY: And somewhere out there — past the cameras, past the light — in rows of humming glass, the little ones are still napping.
[d101] SAY: The System put a pod on the screen, and you let the segment play.
[d101] SAY: They are still there. Still small. Still waiting for someone to decide they matter more than the moment.
[d101] SAY: That is what the word means. Not the watching — anyone can watch. The oversight is the looking away. The most terrible thing the System ever did, it did by accident, because it was bored. You did it on purpose, because you were busy.
[d101] SAY: The village will tell your story for years. It will be a good story. Listen for the part they don't tell.
[d101] SAY: 💛 You hold Dead Phone. Breathe. The shaking eases. (−6 trauma)
[d101] SAY: 💛 You hold Carpenter's square. Your gifts are not mastered — but they are lived-in, worn smooth by use, and they are with you. The feast was the weapon. (The next feast grants FEASTED until tomorrow's dawn: strikes & abilities uplifted, devotion ×1.5 — richer feasts hit harder.)
